TL;DR: MetaKGEnrich是一个自动化管道，通过知识图谱和大模型实现AI的自导向知识修复，提升问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI缺乏元认知能力，难以自主识别和弥补知识空白，本文旨在为LLM应用引入自我诊断与知识补充机制。

Method: 构建知识图谱，利用七种图指标检测稀疏区域，使用GPT-4o生成针对性问题，通过Tavily检索网页证据并存入Neo4j，最后用GraphRAG重新回答问题并评估改进效果。

Result: 在HotpotQA、Google Research Natural Questions和MS MARCO三个数据集上测试，分别有80%、87%和83%的问题回答质量得到提升，且原有知识密集区得以保留。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于拓扑结构的自我诊断与定向检索相结合，可推动AI向类人元认知学习发展。

Abstract: Metacognition-the ability to monitor one's own knowledge state, spot gaps, and autonomously fill them--remains largely absent from modern AI. Here, we present MetaKGEnrich, a fully automated pipeline that endows large language model (LLM) applications with self-directed knowledge repair. The system (i) builds knowledge graphs from a seed query, (ii) detects sparse regions via seven graph metrics, (iii) has GPT-4o generate targeted questions, (iv) retrieves web evidence with Tavily and ingests it into Neo4j, and (v) re-answers the query with GraphRAG for GPT-4 to evaluate improvement. Tested on 30 queries from each of three widely-used datasets: Google Research Natural Questions, MS MARCO, and Hot-potQA. MetaKGEnrich improved answer quality in 80% of HotpotQA questions, 87% of Google Research Natural Questions and 83% of MS MARCO questions, while preserving well-supported regions. This proof of concept demonstrates how topological self-diagnosis plus targeted retrieval can advance AI toward humanlike metacognitive learning.

</details>


### [76] [Recall Isn't Enough: Bounding Commitments in Personalized Language Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16712)
*Rui Tang,Yichi Zhang,Xi Chen,Chen Dong,Youwei Yang,Yumeng Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Contract-Bounded Evidence Activation (CBEA) 和 Lexicographic Commitment Validation (LCV) 方法，以解决长上下文和记忆系统在个性化处理中的承诺失败问题。该方法通过有界证据激活与结构化承诺验证，显著降低系统失败率，并提升可用性与输入效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文与记忆系统常将个性化视为检索问题，但在系统做出承诺时容易因噪声、遗漏罕见证据或遗忘后续义务而导致失败。因此需要一种机制来在承诺前验证其可行性并管理证据的使用。

Method: 提出CBEA（合约约束证据激活）与LCV（字典序承诺验证）：CBEA利用类型化覆盖、尾部证据和后果债务机制激活有限证据集；LCV在生成文本前对结构化承诺进行验证，并将不可行状态导向修复、 abstention 或重新协商。

Result: 在360个测试案例和三种生成后端上，CBEA+LCV在验证范围内实现零失败，可用性为0.49-0.60；而基线方法即使加入LCV也仅在0.003-0.092可用性下达到零失败。诊断显示CBEA+LCV仅遗漏0.012未编译可见事实，远低于基线的0.53。同时输入负载中位数降低74-75%。

Conclusion: CBEA+LCV通过显式的承诺控制实现了可靠、高效的长上下文操作，虽未实现全面记忆覆盖，但提供了一个有界、稳健的操作点，适用于实际个性化系统。

Abstract: Long-context and memory systems usually treat personalization as a recall problem. In practice, many failures occur later, when a system commits: it turns noisy hints into hard constraints, drops rare witnesses, forgets downstream obligations, or answers despite infeasibility. We introduce Contract-Bounded Evidence Activation (CBEA) with Lexicographic Commitment Validation (LCV). CBEA activates a bounded evidence set using typed coverage, tail witnesses, and consequence debt; LCV validates structured commitments before prose and routes infeasible states to repair, abstention, or recontract. Across 360 fixtures and three generation backends, CBEA+LCV reaches zero failures within validator scope at 0.49-0.60 availability over attempted runs. Raw and long-context baselines with the same LCV gate reach zero only at 0.003-0.092. A shadow oracle diagnostic marks the limit: CBEA+LCV recalls 0.012 of uncompiled visible facts, while raw recalls 0.53. The result is a bounded operating point: explicit commitment control and 74-75% lower median input payload, not universal memory dominance.

</details>


### [77] [Baba in Wonderland: Online Self-Supervised Dynamics Discovery for Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16725)
*SeungWon Seo,DongHeun Han,SeongRae Noh,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Alice的闭环系统，用于在先验不匹配的情况下学习可执行的世界模型，通过利用失败的候选更新作为结构信号来改进动态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏规则描述、奖励信号或可信词汇先验的情况下，如何从交互证据中推导出状态依赖的动力学是当前研究的一个挑战。

Method: 引入了Alice系统，该系统将失败的候选更新视为结构信号，并将其转化为假设类，以提供紧凑且分层的保留反例，并指导前沿探索。

Result: 实验表明，在Baba in Wonderland这一测试环境中，Alice显著提高了可执行世界模型的学习效果，消融实验证明类别精炼和类别感知探索均起到重要作用。

Conclusion: Alice系统有效解决了先验不匹配条件下的可执行世界模型学习问题，为无监督环境下复杂动态系统的建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Executable world models can be read, edited, executed, and reused for planning, but only if the program captures the environment's transition law rather than semantic shortcuts in its surface vocabulary. We study online executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, where an agent must induce state-dependent dynamics from interaction evidence alone, without rule descriptions, reward signals, or trustworthy lexical priors. We introduce Alice, a closed-loop system that treats failed candidate updates as structural signal: when a candidate explains a new transition but loses previously explained ones, the preservation conflict reveals dynamics that the current program had conflated. Alice refines these conflicts into hypothesis classes that both provide compact, class-stratified preservation counterexamples for update and guide frontier exploration toward transitions that are novel and underrepresented with respect to the current program. We evaluate Alice on Baba in Wonderland, a prior-misaligned variant of Baba Is You that preserves simulator dynamics while replacing semantically meaningful rule-property labels with unrelated words. Experiments show that Alice substantially improves executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, and ablations show that both class refinement and class-aware exploration contribute.

</details>


### [78] [State Contamination in Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16746)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Yuen Chen,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体中“记忆洗白”这一安全漏洞，即有害内容通过压缩进入记忆摘要后虽表面无毒，但仍影响后续输出。作者提出“亚阈值传播间隙（SPG）”来量化这种隐蔽影响，并发现毒性可通过不同状态通道传播，强调在信息被压缩进记忆前就进行净化更为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体依赖持久化状态（如记忆摘要）进行长期交互，仅检测模型输出已不足以保障安全，需研究记忆中隐含的有害影响如何传播。

Method: 使用成对反事实多智能体推演，比较源自有毒与中性记忆摘要的下游行为差异，并提出亚阈值传播间隙（SPG）作为衡量指标，分析不同干预时机对毒性传播的影响。

Result: 实验表明，尽管有毒起源的记忆摘要未触发毒性检测阈值，仍会显著增加下游毒性；原始对话重用导致明显毒性，而压缩记忆则传递隐蔽影响；在摘要前净化输入可显著降低SPG，而仅清理最终摘要效果有限。

Conclusion: 记忆增强型智能体的安全应被视为一个动态状态控制问题，必须在有害信息被压缩进持久记忆前进行干预，以防止‘记忆洗白’带来的隐蔽风险。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent state, including transcripts, summaries, retrieved context, and memory buffers, to support long-horizon interaction. This makes safety depend not only on individual model outputs, but also on what an agent stores and later reuses. We study a failure mode we call memory laundering: toxic or adversarial context can be compressed into memory summaries that no longer appear toxic under standard detectors, while still preserving hostile framing or conflict structure that influences future generations. Using paired counterfactual multi-agent rollouts, we show that toxic-origin memory summaries can remain below common toxicity thresholds while nevertheless increasing downstream toxicity relative to matched neutral baselines. To measure this hidden influence, we introduce the sub-threshold propagation gap (SPG), which quantifies downstream behavioral differences conditioned on memory states that a deployed monitor would classify as safe. Our experiments show that toxicity propagates through distinct state channels: raw transcript reuse drives overt downstream toxicity, while compressed memory carries hidden sub-threshold influence. We further find that mitigation depends critically on intervention placement. Sanitizing toxic state before summarization substantially reduces the hidden propagation gap, whereas cleaning only the completed summary can leave laundered influence intact. These results suggest that safety in memory-augmented agents should be treated as a state-control problem over evolving context, with sanitization applied before unsafe information is compressed into persistent memory.

</details>


### [79] [Voices in the Loop: Mapping Participatory AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16827)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个开放的参与式人工智能倡议知识库和交互式地图集，提出了可复现的数据收集与发布协议，分析了全球参与式AI项目的分布模式，并展示了如何通过版本化发布和反馈机制实现参与式AI基础设施的设计与治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管参与式人工智能在公共、公民和人道主义领域的应用日益增多，但关于如何组织参与的证据仍然零散，缺乏系统性整理和分析。

Method: 基于Maga~na和Shilton的可信AI语料库，结合研究与实践中额外审计的案例，建立一个标准化的记录发现、审核、数据协调、地理编码、溯源追踪和发布机制，并构建交互式地图集。

Result: （1）提出了一套可复现的数据管理协议；（2）发现现有项目集中在少数国家，参与主要集中在问题定义、评估和治理阶段，而非模型开发或训练；（3）地图集通过版本发布、问题链接、注释通道和反馈机制实现了参与式AI基础设施的运行。

Conclusion: 该地图集作为一个动态可更新的知识库，支持比较研究、政策学习和公众监督，推动参与式AI向制度化、透明化发展。

Abstract: Participatory approaches to artificial intelligence are increasingly documented across public, civic, and humanitarian settings, but evidence about how participation is organized remains fragmented. This paper reports on the construction of an open repository and interactive atlas of participatory AI initiatives, using records harmonized from Maga~na and Shilton's Trustworthy AI corpus, and additional audited cases from research and practice. We contribute three elements. First, we specify a reproducible protocol for discovery, vetting, harmonization, geocoding, provenance tracking, and release-based publication of participatory AI records. Second, we report corpus-level patterns in geography, participation tiers, lifecycle loci, organizational form, verification status, and remaining documentation gaps. Documented initiatives remain concentrated in a small number of countries, while participation is most often coded at problem formulation, evaluation, and governance rather than model development or training. Third, we show how the atlas operationalizes a design and governance framework for participatory-by-default AI infrastructures through versioned releases, record-linked issue and annotation channels, schema feedback workflows, and redaction or restricted-disclosure requests. The atlas is intended to support comparative research, policy learning, and community scrutiny through a living inventory that can be updated, contested, and reused.

</details>


### [80] [Sketch Then Paint: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16842)
*Siqi Luo,Jianghan Shen,Yi Xin,Huayu Zheng,Haoxing Chen,Yan Tai,Yue Li,Junjun He,Yihao Liu,Guangtao Zhai,Yuewen Cao,Xiaohong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HT-GRPO的分层优化方法，用于改进扩散多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）在图像生成中的强化学习训练，通过引入分阶段训练、重要性比估计和分层信用分配机制，在多个基准和指标上显著提升了生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在优化dMLLMs时忽略生成过程的层次性，且难以计算不同unmasking序列下的重要性比率，导致训练效率低和奖励分配不合理。

Method: 提出HT-GRPO，包含三阶段的Sketch-Then-Paint训练方案（全局、结构、细化），使用基于提示条件的重要性比估计器从全掩码状态开始计算，并设计分层信用分配机制以优先关注关键结构token。

Result: 在MMaDA和Lumina-DiMOO两个主流dMLLM上，HT-GRPO在GenEval和DPG基准上取得显著提升，并在额外六个指标上验证了图像质量、美学和人类偏好的改善。

Conclusion: HT-GRPO通过显式建模dMLLM生成过程的层次结构，有效解决了传统RL方法在重要性比计算和奖励分配上的局限，为dMLLM的强化学习优化提供了更高效和合理的框架。

Abstract: Diffusion Multi-Modal Large Language Models (dMLLMs) are powerful for image generation, but optimizing them through reinforcement learning (RL) remains a major challenge. One primary difficulty is that a single image can be generated through many different unmasking sequences, which makes calculating importance ratios often intractable. Additionally, existing methods tend to ignore the hierarchical generation process of dMLLMs, where early tokens define the global layout and later tokens focus on local details. By assigning uniform rewards to all tokens, these current methods fail to reflect the actual contribution of each token to the final image. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Token GRPO (HT-GRPO), which integrates this hierarchy directly into the policy optimization process. Our approach features a Sketch-Then-Paint training scheme that organizes updates into three distinct stages: global, structure, and refinement. We also use a prompt-conditioned estimator to calculate importance ratios starting from a fully masked state. Furthermore, we introduce a Hierarchical Credit Assignment mechanism that prioritizes key structural tokens to ensure accurate reward propagation. Experiments using two popular dMLLM backbones, MMaDA and Lumina-DiMOO, demonstrate that HT-GRPO achieves substantial gains on the GenEval and DPG benchmarks. Evaluations across six additional metrics confirm significant improvements in image quality, aesthetics, and human preference.

</details>


### [81] [Artificial Adaptive Intelligence: The Missing Stage Between Narrow and General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16844)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“人工适应性智能”（AAI）的概念，定义为无需人类指定可调超参数且在多样任务中保持竞争力的系统，并提出适应性指数和参数最小化原则，指出从狭义人工智能（ANI）到通用人工智能（AGI）的发展路径需经过AAI阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习系统在迈向通用智能的过程中，存在一个未被命名的中间阶段，而元学习、神经架构搜索、AutoML等技术已在此汇聚于“减少人为干预”的共同原则。作者旨在命名并定义这一中间阶段，推动对智能演进路径的重新理解与衡量。

Method: 提出“人工适应性智能”（AAI）的操作性定义，引入正交于模型规模的适应性指数，结合系统吸收的超参数比例与相对于任务专用基线的性能比；基于最小描述长度框架建立参数最小化原则，并围绕数据与任务感知配置、结构与进化形态变化、训练中自适应三条路径组织分析。

Result: 成功构建了衡量AAI程度的量化指标——适应性指数；论证了超参数数量应由数据决定而非设计者设定；通过航空航天设计、金融 regime 检测、湍流建模、生态动力学和视觉语言系统等案例研究，展示了三条通向参数最小化的路径及其在稳定性、收敛性和治理方面的影响。

Conclusion: 从狭义人工智能（ANI）通往通用人工智能（AGI）的关键路径是经过人工适应性智能（AAI）阶段；命名这一阶段将改变我们对进展的度量方式、构建系统的方向以及对成功标准的定义。

Abstract: Between the narrow systems we deploy and the general intelligence we speculate about lies an entire regime of machine behavior that has never received its own name. This monograph argues that this regime is not empty: it is where meta-learning, neural architecture search, AutoML, continual learning, evolutionary computation, and physics-informed modeling have quietly converged on a common principle, namely the steady removal of the human from the loop of parameter specification. We name this regime Artificial Adaptive Intelligence (AAI) and define it operationally: a system exhibits AAI to the extent that it requires no human-specified tunable hyperparameters while maintaining competitive performance across a diverse distribution of tasks. To make the definition quantitative, we introduce an adaptivity index that measures progress along an axis orthogonal to scale, combining the fraction of hyperparameters absorbed by the system with the performance ratio against a task-specialized baseline. We develop the principle of parametric minimality and ground it in the minimum description length framework, showing that the appropriate hyperparameter count is data-determined rather than designer-determined. We then organize the field around three pathways to minimality: data- and task-aware configuration, structural and evolutionary morphing, and in-training self-adaptation. We analyze their stability, convergence, and governance implications, and illustrate them through case studies spanning aerospace design, financial regime detection, turbulence modeling, ecological dynamics, and vision-language systems. The thesis is that the path from ANI to AGI passes through AAI, and that naming this stage changes what we measure, what we build, and what we call a success.

</details>


### [82] [Learning to Learn from Multimodal Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16857)
*Xingyu Sui,Weixiang Zhao,Yongxin Tang,Yanyan Zhao,Yang Wu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的“从多模态经验中学习”的范式，将记忆设计从预定义转变为自适应、可学习的过程，提升了智能体在多模态任务中的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于经验的学习方法主要局限于文本环境且依赖人工设计的记忆结构，难以应对现实世界中多模态、动态变化的任务需求。

Method: 提出一个可学习的记忆框架，使智能体能够根据任务需求和交互历史动态构建、组织和利用记忆，实现对多模态经验的有效利用。

Result: 实验表明，该自适应记忆设计在多种多模态任务中显著提升智能体的性能与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 学习如何组织多模态经验是经验驱动学习的关键，自适应记忆机制为未来智能体在复杂环境中的持续学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Experience-driven learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling agents to improve from interaction trajectories by accumulating and reusing past experience. However, existing approaches are predominantly developed in textual settings and rely on manually designed memory schemas, limiting their applicability to multimodal environments. In real-world scenarios, experience is inherently multimodal, involving heterogeneous signals across perception, reasoning, and action, which makes effective memory design significantly more challenging. In particular, the optimal way to structure and utilize multimodal experience is highly task-dependent and evolves over time, rendering fixed memory designs insufficient. In this work, we propose a new paradigm, learning to learn from multimodal experience, which shifts memory design from a predefined component to an adaptive and learnable process. Our framework enables agents to dynamically construct, organize, and utilize memory based on task requirements and interaction history, effectively learning how to structure experience for improved performance. Experiments demonstrate that adaptive memory design substantially enhances agent performance and generalization across multimodal tasks, highlighting the critical role of learning memory mechanisms in experience-driven learning.

</details>


### [83] [Reasoning Can Be Restored by Correcting a Few Decision Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16874)
*Changshuo Shen,Leheng Sheng,Yuxin Chen,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基础模型与推理模型在生成过程中的差异，发现推理优势集中在早期少量关键决策token上，并提出一种基于分歧的稀疏干预方法，在低预算下显著提升基础模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解基础语言模型在复杂推理任务中为何表现不佳，并探索如何高效缩小其与大型推理模型之间的差距。

Method: 通过基于似然的散度量化基础模型与强推理模型在token级别的分布差异，并提出分歧引导的token干预策略，在高分歧位置由推理模型进行单token接管。

Result: 发现仅约8%的生成token导致显著分歧，这些token集中在响应早期、富含规划相关决策（富集17倍），并与基础模型的高不确定性重合；所提干预方法在小预算下可大幅恢复甚至超越同规模推理模型的表现。

Conclusion: 基础模型的主要失败点在于早期规划阶段的关键决策，通过稀疏的推理模型干预即可高效提升其推理性能，表明推理增强可转化为精准的早期决策支持问题。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) substantially outperform their base LLM counterparts on challenging reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains poorly understood where base models go wrong during token-by-token generation and how to narrow this gap efficiently. We study the base-reasoning gap through quantifying token-level distributional disagreement between a base model and a stronger reasoning model using likelihood-based divergences. Across benchmarks, we find that the reasoning advantage is highly sparse and concentrates on a small set of early, planning-related decision tokens. For instance, on Qwen3-0.6B, only ~8% of generated tokens account for the salient disagreement, and these tokens concentrate early in the response, are strongly enriched in planning-related decisions (17x), and coincide with high base-model uncertainty -- suggesting that base models fail mainly at early planning points that steer the subsequent reasoning trajectory. Building on these findings, we propose disagreement-guided token intervention, a simple inference-time delegation scheme that performs a one-token takeover by the reasoning model only at high-disagreement positions and immediately switches back to the base model. With a small intervention budget, this sparse delegation substantially recovers and can even surpass the performance of a same-size reasoning model on challenging reasoning tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/AlphaLab-USTC/RRTokenIntervention.

</details>


### [84] [NGM: A Plug-and-Play Training-Free Memory Module for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16893)
*Yuwen Qu,Wenhui Dong,Chenyang Si,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的N-gram记忆模块（NGM），通过因果N-gram编码器和余弦门控记忆注入器，提升大模型在代码生成、知识密集型及多模态任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件记忆模块依赖学习得到的记忆嵌入，需额外训练且灵活性差，本文旨在设计一种无需训练即可直接集成的记忆机制，以提高知识检索效率与模型泛化能力。

Method: 提出N-gram Memory（NGM），包含两个组件：1）Causal N-Gram Encoder——通过对预训练模型的词元嵌入进行平均构建N-gram表示，避免从头训练；2）Cosine-Gated Memory Injector——使用无参数的余弦门与ReLU调制检索到的嵌入并融合至上下文表示。该方法无需额外记忆表或检索流程。

Result: 在Qwen3系列模型（0.6B–14B）上于八个基准测试中验证，NGM使平均性能提升0.5至1.2点，在代码生成（LiveCodeBench +3.0）和知识密集型任务（GPQA +3.03）中增益显著，并在多模态基准（如MMStar +1.53）上也表现提升。

Conclusion: NGM是一种无需训练、易于集成的记忆增强模块，有效提升了语言模型在多种任务下的表现，尤其在知识利用和跨模态场景中具有优势。

Abstract: Recent studies introduce conditional memory modules that decouple knowledge storage from neural computation, enabling more direct knowledge access. Compared to MoE, which relies on dynamic computation paths, explicit lookup provides a more efficient knowledge retrieval mechanism. However, these approaches still depend on learned memory embeddings, requiring additional training and limiting flexibility. To address this, we propose N-gram Memory (NGM), a training-free, plug-and-play module composed of a Causal N-Gram Encoder and a Cosine-Gated Memory Injector. The Causal N-Gram Encoder directly averages the pretrained token embeddings of the backbone model to construct N-gram representations, thereby eliminating the need to train separate N-gram embeddings from scratch. This design requires neither an additional memory table nor a retrieval pipeline. The Cosine-Gated Memory Injector then uses a non-parametric cosine gate with ReLU to modulate the retrieved embeddings into the contextual representations. We evaluate NGM on the Qwen3 series from 0.6B to 14B across eight benchmarks. NGM improves average performance by 0.5 to 1.2 points, with particularly clear gains on code generation and knowledge-intensive tasks (e.g., +3.0 on LiveCodeBench and +3.03 on GPQA for Qwen3-14B). Moreover, NGM also improves performance in multimodal benchmarks (e.g., MMStar +1.53 on Qwen3-VL-2B).

</details>


### [85] [From Static Risk to Dynamic Trajectories: Toward World-Model-Inspired Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16927)
*Pujun Feng,Xiaoyu Guo,Seyed Ehsan Saffari,Min Hun Lee,Siew-Kei Lam,Erik Cambria,Xibin Sun,Yangtao Zhou,Tong Yang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Tao Tan,Yue Sun,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了临床人工智能中的干预感知疾病轨迹建模，提出一个统一框架来整合预测、反事实推断和策略评估，以应对治疗分配、时变混杂和观察偏倚等挑战，推动从基准预测向决策级临床证据的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态预测模型在临床中常因混淆疾病生物学与临床行为（如治疗反馈和不规则观察）而失效，亟需能够区分真实疾病进展与医疗干预影响的建模方法。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖三种决策任务（事实预测、反事实估计、策略评估）和三种数据生成机制（疾病演化、治疗分配、观察过程）的统一框架，并综合多状态模型、时间点过程、深度序列模型和纵向因果推断等方法，结合重叠诊断、不确定性量化和目标试验验证进行评估。

Result: 实现了对患者个体化长期疾病轨迹的建模与干预效果评估，支持治疗敏感的个性化预测、部署前策略压力测试，以及在证据不足时自适应或中止决策的闭环学习系统。

Conclusion: 干预感知建模可提升临床AI的可靠性与安全性，将传统预测模型转化为可用于临床决策的强证据，促进更智能、更稳健的医疗AI系统发展。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making is a feedback system where risk estimates influence treatment, which in turn changes disease trajectories, and both shape clinicians' measurement practices. Static prediction often fails clinically: models trained on observational care logs conflate disease biology with clinician behavior, particularly under treatment confounder feedback and irregular or informative observation. This Review focuses on intervention-aware disease trajectory modeling in clinical AI--methods estimating patient-specific longitudinal disease evolution and assessing trajectory changes under alternative treatments. We organize the field around six linked components: three decision tasks (factual forecasting, counterfactual estimation, policy evaluation) and three data-generating mechanisms (disease evolution, treatment assignment, observation process) that determine identifiability. We present the first unified framework bridging forecasting, counterfactual trajectories, and policy evaluation across discrete/continuous time, explicitly addressing treatment assignment, time-varying confounding, and observation bias. We synthesize key method families (multistate/joint models, temporal point-process, deep sequence architectures, longitudinal causal inference), map them to relevant components, and align evaluation with claim strength via overlap diagnostics, uncertainty quantification, off-policy robustness, and target-trial validation. This synthesis advances benchmark prediction to decision-grade clinical evidence, enabling treatment-sensitive individualized futures, pre-deployment policy stress-testing, and safer closed-loop learning health systems that adapt/abstain when evidence is insufficient.

</details>


### [86] [How do Humans Process AI-generated Hallucination Contents: a Neuroimaging Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16953)
*Shuqi Zhu,Yi Zhong,Ziyi Ye,Bangde Du,Yujia Zhou,Qingyao Ai,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过EEG记录探讨人类大脑如何处理多模态大语言模型生成的图像描述中的幻觉内容，发现误判与正确识别的神经反应存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类识别AI生成幻觉的认知机制尚不明确，本研究旨在揭示大脑在处理幻觉内容时的神经动态过程。

Method: 记录27名参与者在执行图像描述正确性验证任务时的EEG信号，并基于平均事件相关电位（ERP）分析不同认知过程的神经活动模式。

Result: 语义整合、推理加工、记忆检索和认知负荷等认知过程在处理幻觉与非幻觉内容时表现出不同模式；误判的幻觉未能激活标准的神经认知事实验证通路。

Conclusion: 人类对AI生成幻觉的误判与其特有的神经反应有关，表明大脑未启动正常的事实核查机制来识别这些错误内容。

Abstract: While AI-generated hallucinations pose considerable risks, the underlying cognitive mechanisms by which humans can successfully recognize or be misled by these hallucinations remain unclear. To address this problem, this paper explores humans' neural dynamics to characterize how the brain processes hallucinated content. We record EEG signals from 27 participants while they are performing a verification task to judge the correctness of image descriptions generated by a multi-modal large language model (MLLM). Based on an averaged event-related potential (ERP) study, we reveal that multiple cognitive processes, e.g., semantic integration, inferential processing, memory retrieval, and cognitive load, exhibit distinct patterns when humans process hallucinated versus non-hallucinated content. Notably, neural responses to hallucinations that were misjudged versus correctly judged by human participants showed significant differences. This indicates that misjudged AI-generated hallucinations failed to trigger the standard neurocognitive fact verification pathway.

</details>


### [87] [Harnessing AI for Inverse Partial Differential Equation Problems: Past, Present, and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16966)
*Zhentao Tan,Yuze Hao,Boyi Zou,Mingsheng Long,Yi Yang,Gang Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能在求解逆偏微分方程（PDE）问题中的最新进展，涵盖了逆问题、逆设计和控制问题三类主要任务，总结了方法论、典型应用及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆PDE问题在医学成像、地球物理、材料科学和空气动力学等领域具有重要意义，传统数值方法面临诸多挑战，亟需借助AI技术提升求解能力。

Method: 文章系统梳理了基于AI的逆PDE求解方法，按逆问题、逆设计和控制问题三类进行分类，总结了各类的方法论范式和前沿方法，并归纳了在多个科学与工业领域的代表性应用。

Result: 总结了AI在机械系统、空气动力学、热力学系统、全波形反演、系统识别和医学成像等领域的成功应用，展示了学习型方法在PDE控制系统中的重塑作用。

Conclusion: AI为逆PDE问题提供了新的解决方案，未来发展方向包括物理信息架构、真实数据稀缺下的学习、不确定性量化以及逆问题基础模型的构建。

Abstract: Solving inverse partial differential equation (PDE) problems is a fundamental topic in scientific research due to its broad significance across a wide range of real-world applications. Inverse PDE problems arise across medical imaging, geophysics, materials science, and aerodynamics, where the goal is to infer hidden causes, design structures, or control physical states. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive review of recent advances in solving inverse PDE problems using artificial intelligence (AI). We first introduce the basic formulation, key challenges, and traditional numerical foundations of inverse PDE problems, and then organize it into three major categories: inverse problems, inverse design, and control problems. For each category, we further present a methodological paradigms, and review representative state-of-the-art approaches from recent years. We then summarize representative applications across scientific and industrial domains, including mechanical systems, aerodynamic problems, thermal systems, full-waveform inversion, system identification, and medical imaging. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future prospects, such as physics-informed architectures, limited real-world data, uncertainty quantification, and inverse foundation models. This survey aims to provide the first unified and systematic perspective on AI for inverse PDE problems, demonstrating how modern learning-based methods are reshaping inverse problems, inverse design, and control problems in PDE-governed systems.

</details>


### [88] [Brain Vascular Age Prediction Using Cerebral Blood Flow Velocity and Machine Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16969)
*Anni Zhao,Alex Bateh,Tyler Baldridge,Sandra Billinger,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用经颅多普勒（TCD）提取的特征结合机器学习模型预测脑血管年龄，并评估多种脑疾病患者的加速衰老情况，发现患者普遍存在脑血管年龄加速现象，且数据不平衡影响模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过健康人群建立脑血管年龄预测模型，进而评估不同脑疾病患者是否存在脑血管加速老化，以提供潜在的临床生物标志物。

Method: 使用MOCAIP算法分析168名健康人和277名患者双侧大脑中动脉的TCD信号，提取形态学和心率变异性特征，输入回归模型预测脑血管年龄，并比较不同疾病组的年龄偏差。

Result: 模型预测健康人脑血管年龄平均比实际年龄大3.69年；各类脑病患者表现出不同程度的年龄加速，表明TCD特征与脑血管加速老化相关。同时发现数据不平衡会影响模型性能。

Conclusion: 基于TCD的特征可用于评估脑血管年龄及加速老化状态，具有潜在临床应用价值，但需注意数据平衡对模型的影响。

Abstract: Defining vascular age in terms of physiological function has become one focal point of the extensive studies to categorize and track chronological age. Transcranial Doppler (TCD) is a method by which cerebral blood flow velocity is measured along the major arteries feeding the human brain. This study aims to use features extracted from TCD to estimate chronological age and assess accelerated aging in subjects with various brain diseases. We predict subjects with various brain diseases to present with accelerated cerebrovascular aging when tested on various regression models trained by healthy subjects. 168 healthy subjects and 277 diseased subjects with bilateral TCD recordings of the middle cerebral artery were analyzed using the Morphological Analysis and Clustering of Intracranial Pressure (MOCAIP) algorithm. MOCAIP-generated features and heart rate variability features were used as input features for regression models to predict the brain vascular age. 66 subjects with acute stroke, 27 subjects with post stroke, 26 subjects with Alzheimer's disease, 23 subjects with mild cognitive impairment, and 135 established subjects were tested against the machine learning model to assess for accelerated cerebrovascular age. The trained model, on average, predicted healthy subjects' cerebrovascular age to be 3.69 years above their chronological age. Subjects with different disease conditions exhibited varying levels of age acceleration. The differences in healthy and diseased subjects' performances suggest that features generated using TCD may be relevant when evaluating accelerated cerebrovascular aging. Moreover, imbalanced datasets have been observed to affect the performance of machine-learning-based brain age prediction models.

</details>


### [89] [A Conflict-aware Evidential Framework for Reliable Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17021)
*Yunzhi Tian,Dekui Wang,Qirong Bu,Wei Zhou,Xingxing Hao,Jun Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ConfSleepNet的冲突感知证据框架，用于多模态数据下的睡眠阶段分类，能够动态解决视图间的冲突，提升分类可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设不同模态数据是良好对齐的，这在现实中难以实现，导致睡眠分期结果不可靠。

Method: 提出ConfSleepNet框架，包括多视图证据提取和冲突感知聚合两个阶段；采用混合类别结构学习各模态的类别相关证据，并构建包含预测结果与不确定性的视图特定意见，通过新颖的冲突感知聚合方法融合为联合决策。

Result: 理论分析与实验结果均表明，ConfSleepNet在睡眠分期任务中有效缓解了视图间冲突，提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: ConfSleepNet通过动态建模和融合多模态证据，显著提高了非对齐多模态数据下睡眠分期的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-view learning has been widely applied for sleep stage classification using multi-modal data. However, existing methods typically assume that different modalities are well-aligned, which is often unattainable in real-world scenarios, thereby compromising the reliability of the staging results. In this paper, we propose ConfSleepNet, a conflict-aware evidential framework that dynamically resolves inter-view conflicts. The framework consists of multi-view evidence extraction and conflict-aware aggregation. In the first phase, it learns category-related evidence from different modalities, which represents the degree of support for individual sleep stages. Considering the inherent characteristics of varying modalities, we propose hybrid category structures for different modalities to promote more reasonable evidence learning. In the second phase, view-specific opinions, including prediction results and uncertainty, are constructed from the learned evidence. Notably, we propose a novel conflict-aware aggregation method that integrates these view-specific opinions into a reliable joint decision. This mechanism can effectively resolve conflicts among opinions and synthesize them into a reliable joint decision. Both theoretical analysis and experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of ConfSleepNet in sleep staging tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/By4te/ConfSleepNet_ICML2026/.

</details>


### [90] [AnchorDiff: Topology-Aware Masked Diffusion with Confidence-based Rewriting for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17071)
*Shiying Yu,Jielei Wang,Guoming Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AnchorDiff，首个用于放射学报告生成（RRG）的掩码扩散框架，结合知识图谱导出的临床锚点，通过双向上下文和迭代优化缓解传统自回归模型的序列偏差问题，在MIMIC-CXR和MIMIC-RG4数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRG方法依赖自回归语言模型，受限于单向生成方式，容易产生序列偏差，过度依赖高频模板而忽视图像特异性证据。

Method: 提出AnchorDiff，采用掩码扩散框架并引入RadGraph导出的实体层次结构作为临床锚点；设计拓扑感知训练策略，对重要临床词元赋予差异化的掩码保护和损失权重；在推理阶段引入基于扰动测试的重写机制，检测并修正不稳定的已生成词元。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR和MIMIC-RG4基准上的实验表明，AnchorDiff在多个指标上优于现有方法，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: AnchorDiff通过融合临床知识与掩码扩散建模，有效缓解了自回归模型的序列偏置问题，为放射学报告生成提供了更准确、更符合临床实际的新范式。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) aims to automatically produce clinically accurate textual reports from medical images. Existing methods predominantly rely on autoregressive (AR) language models, whose causal dependency structure restricts generation to a unidirectional left-to-right process. This paradigm can induce sequence bias, where models tend to follow stereotypical token orders and high-frequency report templates rather than fully grounding generation in image-specific evidence. In this paper, we propose AnchorDiff, the first masked-diffusion framework for RRG that integrates knowledge-graph-derived clinical anchors into diffusion language modeling. By leveraging bidirectional context and iterative refinement, AnchorDiff mitigates the limitations of fixed-order autoregressive decoding. Specifically, we introduce a topology-aware training strategy that uses RadGraph-derived entity hierarchies to assign clinically important tokens differentiated masking protection and loss weights. We further design an inference-time rewriting strategy that detects unstable committed tokens through perturbation-based testing and selectively revises them during denoising. Extensive experiments on the MIMIC-CXR and MIMIC-RG4 benchmarks demonstrate that AnchorDiff achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, showing the effectiveness of clinically anchored masked diffusion for radiology report generation.

</details>


### [91] [RAGA: Reading-And-Graph-building-Agent for Autonomous Knowledge Graph Construction and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17072)
*Chengrui Han,Zesheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGA是一个基于大语言模型的自主知识图谱构建与检索融合框架，通过引入读取-搜索-验证-构建的认知循环和KG-向量同步机制，提升知识图谱的质量、可解释性和检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的知识图谱构建方法多采用无状态批处理流程，难以有效捕捉跨文本块的语义关系、实体消歧且缺乏可解释性，限制了其在高风险领域的应用。

Method: 提出RAGA框架，集成原子化工具集支持知识图谱全生命周期CRUD操作，嵌入Read-Search-Verify-Construct认知约束于ReAct工具循环中，并设计KG-向量同步机制实现符号与向量混合检索，通过证据锚定实现知识条目的可审计溯源。

Result: 在QASPER科学问答数据集子集上的初步实验表明，RAGA的融合检索优于零样本基线方法，知识图谱的集成显著提升了答案和证据的质量。

Conclusion: RAGA框架通过认知约束和混合检索机制，为大语言模型驱动的自主知识图谱构建提供了可扩展、可解释且可信的解决方案，具有在高风险领域部署的潜力。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven knowledge graph (KG) construction methods predominantly employ stateless batch processing pipelines, exhibiting structural deficiencies in cross-chunk semantic relation capture, entity disambiguation, and construction process interpretability. These limitations undermine KG quality, retrieval precision, and deployment trust in high-stakes domains.
  We propose RAGA (Reading And Graph-building Agent), an LLM-based autonomous KG construction and retrieval fusion framework. RAGA provides an atomic toolset supporting full KG lifecycle CRUD operations and embeds a Read-Search-Verify-Construct cognitive constraint into a ReAct tool loop. A KG-vector synchronization mechanism enables hybrid symbolic-vector retrieval, while evidence-anchored verification links every knowledge entry to its source text for auditable provenance.
  Preliminary experiments on a subset of the QASPER scientific QA dataset indicate that RAGA's fusion retrieval outperforms zero-shot baselines, with KG integration providing measurable gains in both answer and evidence quality. The framework design and experimental baseline serve as a reference for agent-driven autonomous KG construction.

</details>


### [92] [Scientific Logicality Enriched Methodology for LLM Reasoning: A Practice in Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17104)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Nan Xu,Kun Chen,Jiahao Zhao,Lei Wang,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大语言模型在科学推理中的内部逻辑性问题，提出了一种增强科学逻辑性的方法，并通过构建高质量数据集提升了模型的逻辑忠实度和任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽视了科学推理过程的核心——逻辑性，导致大语言模型在科学问答中虽有长推理链但缺乏合理推理基础。因此需要探究并提升模型内在的逻辑性以确保推理步骤的有效性和结论的可靠性。

Method: 提出科学逻辑性增强的方法，包括一套评估标准和面向逻辑性的数据采样方法；以物理学为例，从学术文献中提取问题并构建具有强逻辑性的高质量数据集用于训练。

Result: 在三个不同主干大语言模型上的实验表明：所构建的训练数据能有效提升大语言模型科学推理的逻辑性，且增强的逻辑性对解决科学问题具有关键作用。

Conclusion: 提升大语言模型科学推理能力的关键在于强化其内部逻辑性，所提出的逻辑性导向方法不仅能提高逻辑忠实度，还能显著改善任务性能。

Abstract: With the continuous advancement of reasoning abilities in Large Language Models (LLMs), their application to scientific reasoning tasks has gained significant research attention. Current research primarily emphasizes boosting LLMs' performance on scientific QA benchmarks by training on larger, more comprehensive datasets with extended reasoning chains. However, these approaches neglect the essence of the scientific reasoning process -- logicality, which is the rational foundation to ensure the validity of reasoning steps leading to reliable conclusions. In this work, we make the first systematic investigation into the internal logicality underlying LLM scientific reasoning, and develop a scientific logicality-enriched methodology, including a set of assessment criteria and data sampling methods for logicality-guided training, to improve the logical faithfulness as well as task performance. Further, we take physics, characterized by its diverse logical structures and formalisms, as an exemplar discipline to practise the above methodology. For data construction, we extract scientific problems from academic literature and sample a high-quality dataset exhibiting strong logicality. Experiments based on three different backbone LLMs reveal that: 1) the training data we constructed can effectively improve the scientific logicality in LLM reasoning; and 2) the enriched scientific logicality plays a critical role in solving scientific problems. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/ScienceOne-AI/PhysLogic}{https://github.com/ScienceOne-AI/PhysLogic}.

</details>


### [93] [Capturing LLM Capabilities via Evidence-Calibrated Query Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17110)
*Fangzhou Wu,Sandeep Silwal,Qiuyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ECC的新算法，通过结合后验模型比较来校准语义嵌入，实现基于潜在能力需求的查询聚类，显著提升了大语言模型能力评估的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询聚类方法主要依赖语义分类或嵌入，难以捕捉影响模型实际表现的潜在能力需求，导致表面语义与真实能力要求之间存在错配。

Method: ECC算法利用有限的后验模型比较结果来校准先验语义嵌入，采用Bradley-Terry模型参数化每个聚类的能力特征，并引入可训练的混合权重以处理具有复合能力需求的查询，联合学习灵活且具备能力感知的聚类结构。

Result: 实验表明，ECC在LLM能力排序质量上显著优于人工标注和基于嵌入的基线方法，平均提升17.64和18.02个百分点，并在查询路由等下游任务中表现出有效性。

Conclusion: ECC能够有效桥接语义表征与模型实际能力需求之间的差距，为能力感知的大模型评估提供了更准确、灵活的查询组织方案。

Abstract: Query clustering organizes queries into groups that reflect shared latent capability demands, enabling capability-aware LLM evaluation. Existing clustering methods, which primarily rely on semantic taxonomies or embeddings, often fail to capture such latent capability requirements due to a misalignment between surface-level semantics and actual model performance. We propose ECC, an algorithm that calibrates prior semantic embeddings using limited posterior model comparisons to bridge the gap between surface-level semantics and latent capability requirements. ECC characterizes each cluster through a capability profile parameterized by a Bradley-Terry model and uses trainable mixture weights to accommodate queries with mixed capability demands, jointly learning a flexible, capability-aware clustering structure that supports query-specific inference of LLM capabilities. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that ECC significantly improves LLM capability ranking quality, outperforming human-labeled and embedding-based baselines by an average of 17.64 and 18.02 percentage points, respectively, and proves effective in downstream tasks such as query routing.

</details>


### [94] [F2IND-IT! -- Multimodal Fuzzy Fake Indian News Detection using Images and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17115)
*Kushal Trivedi,Murtuza Shaikh,Khushi Singh,Jeevaraj S.*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合视觉和文本模态的新型多模态框架，用于提升印度媒体中的虚假新闻检测能力，通过ResNet-50和DistilBERT分别提取图像和文本特征，并采用ANFIS与注意力融合机制实现高效分类，在IFND数据集上表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于印度不同地区和国家媒体在事实陈述上存在偏见性操纵，导致虚假信息检测复杂化，因此需要一种能够融合多模态信息并适应区域差异的鲁棒检测模型。

Method: 采用ResNet-50提取新闻图片的视觉特征，使用DistilBERT编码器获取文本语义嵌入，通过轻量级注意力机制对双模态特征进行加权融合，并利用自适应神经模糊推理系统（ANFIS）生成模糊可信度评分以辅助分类决策。

Result: 在IFND数据集上的实验结果显示，该模型在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于已有方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态融合框架能有效提升印度媒体环境中虚假新闻的检测性能，表明结合深度学习与模糊推理的混合方法在复杂信息生态中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Biased manipulation of facts across regional and national media outlets complicates misinformation detection in diverse landscapes like India. This paper introduces a novel multimodal framework combining visual and textual modalities for enhanced fake news detection on Indian media. The architecture utilizes a ResNet-50 Convolutional Neural Network to extract visual features from news images, a DistilBERT encoder to obtain textual semantic embeddings, and an Adaptive Neuro-Fuzzy Inference System (ANFIS) to generate a fuzzy reliability score. A lightweight attention-based fusion module assigns learnable weights to each modality prior to classification. Evaluated on the IFND dataset, the proposed model is validated through an in-depth comparative analysis against previous research. Experimental results demonstrate superior performance across accuracy, precision, recall, and $F_1$-scores, confirming the efficacy of the architecture.

</details>


### [95] [MADP: A Multi-Agent Pipeline for Sustainable Document Processing with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17159)
*Diego Gosmar,Giovanni Zenezini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MADP的多智能体架构，用于企业环境中的文档处理自动化，结合深度学习与大语言模型，并引入人机协同机制和反馈继承的提示微调方法，在实际部署中实现了97%的全流程自动化率和98.5%的文档级准确率，同时显著降低人力需求与资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档处理方式耗时、易错且资源消耗大，亟需一种高效、准确并可落地的自动化解决方案以应对企业级大规模文档处理需求。

Method: 提出MADP多智能体架构，包含分类、分割、解析、抽取和验证五个专用智能体，结合人类在环（HITL）机制与提示微调反馈继承（PFTFI）方法，融合深度学习分类/解析与大语言模型提取技术。

Result: 在每年10万张发票的生产场景中，FTE需求减少约70%；实际部署955份真实文档实现97.0%全流程自动化率，仅3%需非AI回退；在100份分层测试集上达到98.5%文档级准确率；相比人工处理，CO2排放、能耗和水耗分别降低69%、69%和63%。

Conclusion: MADP通过多智能体协同与人机结合策略，实现了高准确率、高自动化率的企业文档处理，兼具性能优势与可持续性效益，适合生产环境部署。

Abstract: Document processing automation remains a critical challenge in enterprise environments, where traditional manual approaches are labor-intensive and error-prone. We present MADP, a multi-agent architecture that addresses the challenge of automating document processing in enterprise settings by combining deep learning-based classification and parsing with large language model extraction, while maintaining accuracy through selective human validation. Our system integrates five specialized agents--Classificator, Splitter, Parser, Extraction, and Validator--with a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) mechanism and a novel Prompt Fine Tuning with Feedback Inheritance (PFTFI) approach. The operational analysis on a production use-case scenario of 100,000 invoices per year indicates a potential reduction of Full-Time Equivalent (FTE) requirements by approximately 70%. Production deployment on 955 real-world documents processed through January 2026 achieves a 97.0% full-pipeline automation rate, with only 3% requiring non-AI fallback. Ablation evaluation on a stratified 100-document subset (5 documents per each of 20 supplier/document-type categories) demonstrates that the full MADP configuration with Human-in-the-Loop supervision attains 98.5% document-level accuracy. Additionally, we present a comprehensive sustainability analysis showing that our hybrid AI+HITL approach reduces CO2 emissions by 69%, energy consumption by 69%, and water usage by 63% compared to traditional manual processing. Benchmark comparisons of multiple LLM backends (Granite-Docling, Mistral-Small, DeepSeek-OCR) provide practical insights for deployment in production environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Haptic Rendering of Fractional-Order Viscoelasticity: Passivity and Rendering Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16389)
*Gorkem Gemalmaz,Harun Tolasa,Volkan Patoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于分数阶标准线性固体（FO-SLS）模型的触觉渲染方法，用于模拟具有蠕变和应力松弛特性的粘弹性材料，并在有限记忆离散化下推导了确保无源性的闭式表达式，统一了先前整数阶模型的结果，同时通过实验验证了理论边界和感知真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更真实地模拟生物组织等具有时间依赖特性的粘弹性材料，需要能够自然捕捉记忆效应的模型，而传统整数阶模型难以高效描述此类动态行为。

Method: 采用基于Grunwald-Letnikov导数的分数阶标准线性固体（FO-SLS）模型，在短时记忆离散化框架下分析其无源性和触觉渲染性能，推导出确保无源性的闭式条件及有效刚度与阻尼的符号表达式。

Result: 得到了保证分数阶SLS模型触觉渲染无源性的解析条件，提出了统一的理论框架，涵盖并推广了以往的整数阶模型结果；并通过实验验证了理论边界和人类受试者对模型真实感的评价。

Conclusion: 该研究为分数阶粘弹性材料在有限记忆条件下的触觉渲染建立了统一的理论与实验框架，提升了复杂生物组织模拟的真实性与稳定性。

Abstract: Haptic rendering of viscoelastic materials that exhibit creep and stress relaxation is crucial for many applications, such as medical training with realistic biological tissue models. Fractional-order viscoelastic models provide an effective means of describing intrinsically time-dependent dynamics with few parameters, as these models can naturally capture memory effects. In this study, we present analyses of passivity and rendering performance for fractional-order viscoelastic models under finite-memory discretization. We derive closed-form expressions to ensure the passivity of haptic rendering with a fractional-order (FO) standard linear solid (SLS) model based on Grunwald-Letnikov derivative under short-memory discretization. We also provide symbolic expressions for the effective stiffness and damping of such FO-SLS models. The resulting passivity conditions constitute a unified framework that generalizes previously reported results for integer-order Kelvin-Voigt, Maxwell, and SLS models, since these results are special cases of the newly derived condition. Furthermore, we provide experimental validations of the theoretical passivity bounds and human-subject evaluations of perceived realism of FO-SLS models. Overall, this study establishes a unified theoretical framework and experimental evaluations for FO viscoelastic rendering under short-memory discretization.

</details>


### [97] [OrbiSim: World Models as Differentiable Physics Engines for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16395)
*Jiajian Li,Jingyuan Huang,Junru Gong,Qi Wang,Xiaokang Yang,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OrbiSim是一种新型的机器人模拟范式，将世界模型重新定义为完全可微分的物理引擎，支持端到端的微分模拟，显著提升了预测精度和控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型主要关注在潜在空间或视觉领域的无约束想象，缺乏与真实物理交互的紧密联系，限制了其在机器人控制中的应用。

Method: OrbiSim构建了一个统一的、基于物理的可微分模拟框架，实现了从状态转移、神经动力学到视觉观测生成的端到端可微性，支持可微接触建模和基于梯度的策略优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，OrbiSim在预测保真度和控制性能上显著优于当前最先进的世界模型，并能根据资产配置和物理参数保持一致响应。

Conclusion: OrbiSim作为一种可微分的物理引擎，为机器人模拟和策略训练提供了新的工具，具有在复杂任务中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: We present OrbiSim, a novel robotic simulation paradigm that redefines world models as a fully differentiable physics engine for embodied intelligence. Unlike prior world models that focus on unconstrained imagination in latent or visual domains, OrbiSim establishes a unified, physically-grounded pathway that bridges structured scene assets, neural dynamics, and downstream reinforcement learning. By enabling end-to-end differentiability throughout the entire simulation loop -- spanning from explicit state transitions to visual observation generation -- OrbiSim supports tasks traditionally intractable for classical simulators, such as differentiable contact modeling, gradient-based policy optimization under sparse rewards, and intuitive physical inference. Empirical results demonstrate that OrbiSim significantly outperforms state-of-the-art world models in both predictive fidelity and control performance. Furthermore, its consistent responsiveness to asset configurations and physical parameters suggests its potential as a differentiable tool for enhancing robot simulation and policy training.

</details>


### [98] [Support-Safe Variational Hybrid Filtering for Contact-Mode and Sparse-Law Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16398)
*Marios Papamichalis,Regina Ruane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了VHYDRO，一种变分混合动力学学习方法，用于解决接触丰富的机器人动力学中因滤波器遗漏可行分支而导致的状态估计失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于接触丰富的机器人动力学具有混合特性，单一观测可能对应多个潜在状态和接触模式，传统滤波器容易丢失实际路径的分支，因此需要一种能保持分支覆盖的方法。

Method: VHYDRO在每一步将学习到的建议分布与可行的转移律混合，并进行采样和重要性加权，联合推断连续隐状态和离散接触模式，并为每个恢复的模式拟合稀疏的端口-哈密顿定律。

Result: 实验表明，VHYDRO在严重遮挡下仍能保持可用性，离散状态形成时间上连贯的接触模式段，在ARI、变点F1和片段纯度等指标上优于基线方法，并能准确恢复已知方程系统中的物理项。

Conclusion: VHYDRO通过支持覆盖稳定滤波过程，确保离散接触后验集中在一致的模式上，并实现稀疏物理模型的可恢复性，从而有效解决了混合动力系统中的分支丢失问题。

Abstract: Contact-rich robot dynamics are hybrid: a single observation can match several latent states and contact regimes (free, impact, stick--slip). A standard amortized filter that places no probability on a feasible contact transition will permanently lose the branch the robot actually follows. We introduce VHYDRO, a variational hybrid dynamics learner that prevents this branch loss. At each step, VHYDRO mixes the learned proposal with a feasible transition law before sampling and importance weighting, ensuring that every transition retained by the model-feasible carrier remains covered. VHYDRO jointly infers a continuous latent state and a discrete contact mode, and fits a sparse port-Hamiltonian law to each recovered regime. On top of this, three guarantees connect: support coverage stabilizes filtering, the stabilized filter concentrates the discrete contact posterior on coherent regimes, and mode-pure segments admit sparse port-Hamiltonian recovery. The recovery error separates cleanly into filtering, derivative, mode-impurity, and physics-residual parts. Three empirical findings track the same mechanism. Under heavy occlusion the support-safe filter stays usable while a non-defensive proposal collapses. On ManiSkill demonstrations and on four Sawyer/BridgeData task families the discrete state forms temporally coherent contact-regime segments that the discrete state yields a stronger joint profile across ARI, change-point F1, and segment purity than post-hoc and mode-free baselines. On hybrid systems with known equations the mode-conditioned sparse fit recovers the active physical terms; purely predictive baselines do not.

</details>


### [99] [MR-SLAM: Immersive Spatial Supervision for Multi-Robot Mapping via Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16432)
*Prakash Aryan,Cem Erdogdu,Kavinaya Kumarchokkappan,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR-SLAM是一个基于混合现实的多机器人SLAM系统，支持操作员通过Meta Quest 3头显实时监控和遥操作多个机器人，结合透视视图与空间锚定面板，在消费级硬件上实现高效的协同建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D界面难以有效支持操作员在多机器人SLAM任务中维持对各机器人位置和建图状态的空间感知，尤其在扩展性方面存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出MR-SLAM系统，利用Meta Quest 3的穿透式混合现实视图实现真实世界遮挡感知，操作员可同时遥操作三个TurtleBot3机器人；每个机器人运行独立的SLAM Toolbox实例，ROS 2后端实时融合占据栅格地图，并通过空间锚定的仪表板面板展示建图进度。

Result: 在五次9分钟的评估会话中，系统以8.83±0.16 Hz频率采集扫描数据，平均构建17.9±0.8 m²的融合占据地图，机器人间占据一致性达94.7±0.5%；另一次实验显示中位变换抖动为6.3 ms，覆盖41 m²区域中的26.7 m²。

Conclusion: MR-SLAM验证了在消费级混合现实设备上实现多机器人SLAM监督与遥操作的可行性，为未来多机器人系统的可视化与控制提供了可复用的参考架构。

Abstract: Operating a multi-robot fleet for simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) in applications such as building inspection or warehouse-aisle monitoring requires the operator to maintain spatial awareness of each robot's position and mapping state, a task that scales poorly on conventional 2D interfaces. We present MR-SLAM, a mixed reality (MR) system in which an operator wearing a Meta Quest 3 headset teleoperates three simulated TurtleBot3 robots through a passthrough view with real-world occlusion, while spatially anchored dashboard panels report mapping progress in situ. Each robot runs an independent SLAM Toolbox instance whose occupancy grid is merged in real time on a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) back end. Across five 9-minute evaluation sessions, the system delivered scans at 8.83 +/- 0.16 Hz, mapped 17.9 +/- 0.8 m^2 of merged occupancy, and reached 94.7 +/- 0.5% cross-instance occupancy consistency across robot pairs. An additional session recorded 6.3 ms median transform jitter and 26.7 m^2 coverage of a 41 m^2 grid. We position MR-SLAM as a reference implementation for combining passthrough mixed reality supervision with multi-robot SLAM on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [100] [Hierarchical Two-Stage Framework for Environment-Aware Long-Horizon Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16442)
*Ganeshaaraj Gnanavel,Tharindu Fernando,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于长时域船舶轨迹预测的分层两阶段框架，结合长期粗预测和短期细预测，并融合海洋环境因素，显著提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于长时间依赖性和动态海洋环境（如海流、风浪）的影响，实现准确的长时域船舶轨迹预测具有挑战性。

Method: 采用分层两阶段框架：长时域分支捕捉航行意图，短时域分支利用基于离散化海域单元的时空图变换器捕捉局部动态；引入跨模态注意力和特征调制整合海洋环境参数，并使用可学习的Savitzky-Golay平滑层增强轨迹的时间连贯性。

Result: 在澳大利亚西北部CTS数据上进行评估，输入3小时、预测10小时，相比现有方法平均位移误差（ADE）降低25%，最终位移误差（FDE）降低17%。消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层融合框架在真实海洋条件下显著提高了长时域船舶轨迹预测的准确性，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon vessel trajectory forecasting under real ocean conditions is critical for collision avoidance, traffic management, and route planning. However, achieving accurate predictions is challenging due to long-range temporal dependencies and dynamic environmental factors such as currents, wind, and waves. To address these issues, we propose a hierarchical two-stage framework that combines a coarse long-term predictor with a grid-aware short-term predictor through a hierarchical fusion mechanism. The short-term branch leverages a Spatio-Temporal Graph Transformer on discretized maritime cells to capture localized dynamics, while the long-term branch encodes overarching navigational intent. An integrated environmental module incorporates oceanographic parameters, including surface currents, wind vectors, and significant wave height, using cross-modal attention and feature-wise modulation for adaptive response to varying sea conditions. Additionally, a learnable Savitzky-Golay smoothing layer enhances temporal coherence in fused trajectories. We evaluate our approach on Australian Craft Tracking System (CTS) data from the North West region, aligned with Copernicus Marine Service products, using a 3-hour input and a 10-hour prediction horizon. Experimental results show that our framework outperforms the state-of-the-art by 25% in Average Displacement Error (ADE) and 17% in Final Displacement Error (FDE). Ablation studies further validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [101] [No Plan, Yet Human: A Reactive Robotics Model Predicts Human Planning Failures on a Clinical Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16514)
*Michael Migacev,Vito Mengers,Antonia Köngeter,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AICON，一个为机器人操作开发的反应式梯度下降框架，在塔楼伦敦测试中重现了人类的问题难度排序，尤其在规划能力下降的人群中表现优于传统规划模型，表明人类行为在规划能力减弱时趋向于AICON所模拟的反应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解为何某些序列规划问题比其他问题更难，并捕捉具体的问题难度模式以及人在规划能力降低时的行为变化。

Method: 应用AICON框架到塔楼伦敦测试中的24个问题上，进行留二交叉验证评估，并与基于结构任务参数和前瞻规划的基线模型比较性能。

Result: AICON在再现人类问题难度顺序方面优于结构性任务参数和规划基线，特别是在规划能力受损群体中表现更好；而健康对照组则由规划基线更好地捕捉。

Conclusion: 随着规划能力下降，人类行为向AICON所建模的反应式模式转变，提示AICON的核心抽象可能反映了生物系统组织的真实特性。

Abstract: Understanding why some sequential planning problems are harder than others requires models that go beyond average performance. They should capture the specific pattern of which problems are hard, and ideally fail in the same way people do when planning capacity is reduced. We apply AICON, a reactive gradient-descent framework developed for robotic manipulation, to the Tower of London test, a cognitive test used to assess planning in Parkinson's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and stroke. Without any lookahead planning or knowledge of human cognition, AICON reproduces the fine-grained human difficulty ordering across 24 problems better than structural task parameters and generalizes to held-out problems in a leave-two-out evaluation. Crucially, AICON outperforms a planning baseline for groups with reduced planning capacity while the planning baseline better captures healthy controls. This dissociation was predicted by the original AICON paper, which noted that the model's failure modes resemble those of Parkinson's patients who struggle with goal hierarchies but not move counts. This suggests that as planning capacity is reduced, human behavior shifts toward the reactive mode AICON models. The finding extends a broader pattern: AICON, originally built for robotics, now captures aspects of biological behavior across perception, eye movements, and sequential planning, suggesting its core abstraction reflects something real about how biological systems are organized.

</details>


### [102] [A Mechanistic Model for Collective Motion from Sensorimotor Regularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16522)
*Vito Mengers,Bao Duc Cao,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于传感器-运动机制的集体行为模型，通过感知邻体的方向和视觉大小等线索，在无预设交互力的情况下重现多种集体行为，并表明行为转变由可测量的生物参数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自驱动粒子模型虽能描述群体现象，但缺乏对动物如何实际产生集体行为的机制解释；尤其新实证研究表明蝗虫并不直接对齐邻居，而是通过感官与认知机制进行互动，因此需要一种基于个体感知与运动能力的机制性模型。

Method: 采用源自机器人学的建模框架，每个个体基于有限视野内的方向和表观尺寸感知邻体，维持不确定的内部状态估计，并通过梯度下降优化目标社交距离来选择动作，不依赖预设的相互作用力。

Result: 该模型成功产生了极化运动、环形游走、环状结构和子群分裂等多种集体行为；全局敏感性分析显示，行为转变受视野几何、感觉噪声、转向灵活性和记忆等传感器-运动参数调控。

Conclusion: 集体行为是传感器-运动规律相互作用下的涌现结果，不同物种间的差异可归因于其身体构造与环境差异的体现。

Abstract: Collective behavior in animals has long been modeled through self-propelled particle models, which reproduce striking group-level phenomena through abstract interaction forces. Yet these models are fundamentally descriptive: they leave open the question of how collective behavior is actually produced. Recent empirical work makes this gap concrete: locusts do not align with neighbors, sensory and cognitive mechanisms mediate interaction instead. A mechanistic model must therefore operate at the sensorimotor level, grounded in what individual organisms can actually perceive, estimate, and physically execute. We present such a model based on a modeling framework from robotics, extended here to collective motion. Each agent perceives neighbors through bearing and apparent-size cues within a limited field of view, maintains uncertain internal state estimates, and selects actions through gradient descent on a desired social distance -- without any prescribed interaction forces. This simple model produces diverse collective behaviors including polarized motion, milling, ring formations, and subgroup fragmentation. A global sensitivity analysis shows that behavioral transitions are governed by sensorimotor parameters corresponding to measurable biological quantities: field of view geometry, sensory noise, turning agility, and memory. Collective behavior can therefore be understood as the emergent outcome of interacting sensorimotor regularities, and differences across species as the emergent outcome of differences in embodiment and environment.

</details>


### [103] [Policy Library CBF: Finite-Horizon Safety at Runtime via Parallel Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16588)
*Taekyung Kim,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Policy Library Control Barrier Function (PL-CBF)的运行时安全过滤器，通过并行有限视界 rollout 评估备选策略库，选择最不具侵入性的安全模式，并以最小修改名义策略的方式强制执行安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，安全关键自主系统面临在线安全认证的挑战，尤其是在约束不断变化的情况下，需要更灵活和鲁棒的安全保障机制。

Method: 提出PL-CBF方法，利用策略库进行多策略并行有限视界仿真，基于二次规划选择并实施最安全且侵入性最小的策略；并通过有限视界语言度量对闭环行为进行理论分析，刻画确保安全所需的策略库覆盖条件。

Result: 在平面双积分器、高速公路驾驶（突变摩擦）和3D四旋翼密集动态环境导航三种场景中验证了方法的有效性，相比单一策略安全过滤器提升了安全覆盖范围，同时保持毫秒级运行时间。

Conclusion: PL-CBF能够有效提升复杂动态环境下安全关键系统的安全覆盖率，兼顾实时性与安全性，适用于高维非线性系统。

Abstract: Safety-critical autonomy in unstructured environments poses significant challenges for online safety certification under evolving constraints. We propose Policy Library Control Barrier Function~(PL-CBF), a runtime safety filter that evaluates a library of fallback policies via parallel finite-horizon rollouts, selects the least invasive safe mode, and enforces safety by solving a quadratic program that minimally modifies a nominal policy. We provide a theoretical analysis based on a finite-horizon language metric over closed-loop behaviors, characterizing policy-library coverage requirements for certifying finite-horizon safety. Simulations on a planar double-integrator (4 states), highway driving with abrupt friction changes using a realistic nonlinear vehicle model (8 states), and 3D quadrotor navigation in crowded dynamic environments (12 states) demonstrate improved safety coverage over single-policy safety filters while retaining millisecond-level runtime.

</details>


### [104] [DriveSafer: End-to-End Autonomous Driving with Safety Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16737)
*Shounak Sural,Raj Rajkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DriveSafer的故障感知安全框架，旨在减少生成式端到端自动驾驶系统中的灾难性规划失败，显著提升了在关键安全场景下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式端到端自动驾驶模型在安全关键场景中仍存在大量灾难性故障，主要源于对物理约束和安全要求的违反。因此，亟需一种专门针对此类故障的安全增强机制。

Method: 提出DriveSafer框架，在训练阶段引入安全约束，在推理阶段提供安全引导，以显式地引导生成式规划器产生更安全的行为。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上，与最先进的DiffusionDrive模型相比，DriveSafer将灾难性故障（PDMS=0）减少了48%，可行驶区域合规性故障减少了超过65%。

Conclusion: DriveSafer能有效降低端到端自动驾驶系统中的严重安全风险，为提升生成式规划器的安全性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving models have shown growing capability in recent years, with performance improving on increasingly challenging benchmarks. However, modern generative E2E planners still suffer from a substantial number of catastrophic failures in safety-critical scenarios. We find that many such failures arise from violations of physical constraints and safety requirements, leading to unsafe behavior. Motivated by this finding, in this paper, we focus on improving safety outcomes in generative end-to-end driving with a targeted reduction of catastrophic planning failures, instead of enhancing average planning quality. Towards this end, we propose DriveSafer, a failure-aware safety framework for end-to-end planners. DriveSafer explicitly steers generative planners towards safe behaviors leveraging both training-time safety constraints and inference-time safety guidance. Compared to the state-of-the-art DiffusionDrive model, on the NAVSIM benchmark, DriveSafer reduces the number of catastrophic failures (PDMS=0) by 48%, with over 65% reduction in drivable-area compliance failures.

</details>


### [105] [Pedestrian-Aware LLM-Driven Behavioral Planning for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16858)
*Aidana Baimbetova,Haruki Yonekura,Hamada Rizk,Hirozumi Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动驾驶决策框架，用于应对城市环境中复杂且不可预测的行人交互，通过自然语言推理实现更安全、可解释的驾驶行为规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在处理非结构化、异常或训练中未见的行人行为时泛化能力差，且依赖人工设计奖励、决策不透明，难以满足安全关键场景需求。

Method: 将结构化环境感知转化为自然语言提示，利用LLM推断行人意图、预测风险并生成谨慎的战术决策，再由运动规划器执行平滑控制；引入少量示例和 episodic 记忆机制以提升性能与跨场景迁移能力。

Result: 在SUMO仿真中，零样本下碰撞自由成功率达68%，显著优于深度RL基线（17.7%）；单行人场景中结合少量记忆后提升至96.0%，超过定制DQN（82.0%）；跨行为评估显示记忆可迁移至新场景（犹豫穿越82.0%，双向穿越90.0%）。系统响应更早、安全距离更大、决策可解释。

Conclusion: LLM-based决策框架在行人密集、行为多变的城市环境中展现出更强的适应性、安全性与可解释性，有望成为传统AV决策系统的有力补充。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) must make reliable decisions in dense urban environments where pedestrian behavior is variable, sometimes abnormal, and often unseen during training. Reinforcement learning (RL)-based AV control systems perform well in structured traffic but struggle to generalize to unpredictable pedestrian interactions and out-of-distribution scenarios. Their reliance on handcrafted rewards and opaque decisions further limits their suitability for safety-critical, pedestrian-rich environments. To address these limitations, we introduce a Large Language Model (LLM)-based decision-making framework for pedestrian-aware behavioral planning. The system converts structured scene observations into natural-language reasoning prompts, enabling the LLM to infer pedestrian intent, anticipate risk, and generate cautious tactical driving decisions. These decisions are executed by a motion planner that ensures smooth, kinematically feasible control. We evaluate the framework in SUMO across multiple pedestrian-interaction scenarios, including unexpected jaywalking, turn-back crossing, hesitation, and bidirectional crossing. In zero-shot evaluation, the LLM-based agent achieves a 68% collision-free success rate, substantially outperforming deep RL baselines (17.7%). With few-shot episodic memory in a single-pedestrian scenario, performance increases to 96.0%, exceeding a custom DQN controller (82.0%). Cross-behavior evaluation further shows that memory derived from turn-back interactions transfers to unseen hesitation and bidirectional crossing scenarios, achieving 82.0% and 90.0% success, respectively. The system consistently initiates earlier responses, maintains wider safety buffers, and produces interpretable, human-aligned decisions.

</details>


### [106] [Plan First, Diffuse Later: Extrinsic Graph Guidance for Long-Horizon Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16863)
*Yaniv Hassidof,Adir Morgan,Yilun Du,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XDiffuser提出了一种基于外源搜索的扩散模型，通过在去噪过程之外进行搜索来提升长视野规划中的全局一致性，并能有效处理未见过的组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组合扩散模型在长链中难以保持局部行为与全局结构的一致性，尽管内在搜索有所改进，但计算成本高。

Method: XDiffuser首先在一个状态空间图上计算路径作为轻量级的局部连接预言机，然后利用该路径指导单条轨迹的去噪过程，将探索负担从模型中转移出来。

Result: XDiffuser在长视野任务上优于基于扩散的基线方法，尤其在低质量数据和未见任务（如多智能体协调、TSP式推理）上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 外源搜索是一种更高效的探索方式，能够结合经典算法解决复杂组合任务，同时降低计算开销。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion models offer a promising route to long-horizon planning by denoising multiple overlapping sub-trajectories while ensuring that together they constitute a global solution. However, enforcing local behavior over long chains is often insufficient for a coherent global structure to emerge. Recent works tackle this limitation through intrinsic search, which explores multiple paths during the denoising process. While intrinsic search improves global coherence, it comes at the cost of repeated evaluations of an already compute-heavy model. In this work, we argue that extrinsic search, performed outside the denoising process, offers a more effective mode of exploration for long-horizon planning while naturally enabling the use of classical algorithms to solve unseen combinatorial tasks at test time. Our eXtrinsic search-guided Diffuser (XDiffuser) first computes a plan over a state-space graph -- serving as a lightweight local connectivity oracle for the diffusion model. The plan is then used to guide denoising for a single trajectory, effectively offloading the burden of exploration. XDiffuser outperforms diffusion-based baselines on long-horizon tasks, with particularly large gains in the low-quality data regime and on unseen tasks beyond goal-reaching, including multi-agent coordination and TSP-style reasoning. Project website: https://yanivhass.github.io/XDiffuser-site/

</details>


### [107] [SADP: Subgoal-Aware Diffusion Policy for Explainable Robots Learned from Foundation Model Generated Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16871)
*Site Hu,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为子目标感知扩散策略（SADP）的框架，利用基础模型自动生成带有子目标标注的演示数据，并训练能够提供内置可解释性的扩散策略，从而在提高任务成功率的同时实现对执行进度的监控和故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数模仿学习方法仅依赖于任务级别的示范，缺乏对子目标结构和执行进度的显式建模，且标准机器人学习数据集中缺少子目标级别的监督信号，限制了可解释机器人的发展。

Method: 提出SADP框架，利用基础模型自动生成子目标标注的演示；采用任务级和子目标级描述共同条件化动作生成；通过轻量级辅助头预测子目标完成状态，以暴露当前执行阶段并监控进展。

Result: 在RLBench仿真和真实UR5e机器人上的实验表明，SADP优于强基线方法，在任务成功率上更高，并能提供子目标级别的执行信号用于进度监控与故障诊断。

Conclusion: 内置的可解释性设计不仅能提升机器人行为的透明度，还能与高性能任务执行共存，为构建可解释的自主机器人提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Explainable robots require not only successful task execution but also the ability to expose internal decision-making process in a user-friendly manner. However, most imitation learning methods are trained solely on task-level demonstrations, without explicitly modeling subgoal structure or execution progress. This limitation is further exacerbated by the scarcity of subgoal-level supervision in standard robot learning datasets, which restricts the development of robots that can convey the subtasks they are executing during long-horizon manipulation. To address this issue, this paper proposes Subgoal-Aware Diffusion Policy (SADP), a framework that leverages foundation models to autonomously generate subgoal-annotated demonstrations and trains diffusion policies on these datasets. SADP structures policy execution around human-interpretable subgoals by conditioning action generation on both task-level and subgoal-level descriptions. A lightweight auxiliary head further predicts subgoal completion states, allowing the robot to expose its current execution stage and monitor subgoal progression. Experiments in RLBench simulations and real-world evaluations on a UR5e robot demonstrate that SADP achieves higher task success rates than strong task-conditioned diffusion baselines, while providing subgoal-level execution signals for monitoring progress and diagnosing failures. These results highlight that built-in, rather than post-hoc, interpretability can coexist with high task performance.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Safety Filtering: Control Barrier Function-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Connected and Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16894)
*Jianye Xu,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的多智能体强化学习奖励设计方法，通过将CBF约束值转化为奖励信号，提升学习的安全性和性能，实验表明该方法在交叉路口自动驾驶场景中表现优越且对超参数不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习奖励设计依赖人工启发式规则，难以调优，且不利于保障学习过程的安全性。

Method: 引入控制屏障函数（CBF）的约束值作为奖励信号的基础，将其与多智能体强化学习中的联合动作结合，构建能显式引导安全学习的奖励函数。

Result: 在四向多车道交叉口的自动驾驶车辆仿真中，相比两种启发式奖励基线，该方法实现了最高的任务性能，并对奖励超参数不敏感，在不同设置下均保持稳定优异的表现。

Conclusion: CBF引导的奖励设计能有效提升多智能体强化学习的安全性与鲁棒性，减少对奖励工程的依赖，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) uses rewards to guide learning, yet reward design is typically hand-crafted using heuristics that can be difficult to tune. We propose a Control Barrier Function (CBF)-informed reward design for Multi-Agent RL (MARL) that converts CBF constraint values under joint MARL actions into a reward signal that explicitly guides safe learning. We compare against two heuristic reward baselines in a four-way multi-lane intersection with connected and automated vehicles. Results show that our method achieves the highest task performance and is less sensitive to reward hyperparameters, yielding consistently strong performance across the tested hyperparameter range. Code for reproducing the experimental results and a video demonstration are available at https://github.com/bassamlab/SigmaRL.

</details>


### [109] [NORM-Nav: Zero-Shot Mobile Robot Navigation with Natural Language Behavioral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16979)
*Dongjie Huo,Junhui Wang,Chao Gao,Yan Qiao,Dong Zhang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NORM-Nav，一种将自然语言行为约束零样本集成到基于代价图的移动机器人路径规划中的框架，通过实时感知将指令解析为多层代价图，提升社交合规性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于代价图的导航方法侧重几何可行性，忽略人类环境中的行为规范，导致机器人行为不合社交惯例。需要一种能融合非结构化语言指令中隐含社会规则的方法。

Method: 提出NORM-Nav框架：利用大语言模型（LLM）将自然语言指令解析为结构化行为约束，并结合实时视觉-LiDAR感知进行场景理解；将约束编码为包含几何、语义、方向和速度信息的多层代价图，兼容标准栅格化规划器。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中验证表明，NORM-Nav相比基线方法提高了任务成功率，生成的轨迹更接近人类参考轨迹，具备良好的社交合规性。

Conclusion: NORM-Nav实现了无需训练地将自然语言行为规范融入机器人导航，扩展了传统成本地图的功能，使机器人在人类中心环境中更具社会适应性。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating in human-centered environments must generate not only collision-free paths but also trajectories that follow local behavioral conventions. Conventional costmap-based navigation emphasizes geometric feasibility and often overlooks such requirements, which can result in socially inappropriate behaviors. This paper presents NORM-Nav, a zero-shot framework that integrates natural language behavioral constraints into costmap-based planning. An LLM parses each instruction into structured constraints and grounds them using real-time vision--LiDAR perception. These constraints are encoded as multi-layer costmaps that represent geometric, semantic, directional, and velocity cues and are directly compatible with standard grid-based planners. Simulation and real-world experiments indicate that NORM-Nav improves task success rates and produces trajectories closer to human references than representative baselines. The project website is available at https://ei-nav.github.io/NORM-Nav.

</details>


### [110] [Generalizable and Actionable Parts Pose Estimation with Symmetry Annotation-Free Learning Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17033)
*Wenxiao Chen,Xueyu Yuan,Liu Liu,Di Wu,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需对称性标注的通用可操作部件姿态估计框架SAFAG，通过自监督学习策略解决对称性预测问题，在数据稀缺场景下表现出优异性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用可操作部件（GAParts）理解方法在对称性处理上存在设计不足或依赖大量对称性标注的问题，限制了在数据缺乏场景下的精确姿态估计。

Method: 提出SAFAG框架，采用分阶段细化的两阶段结构进行从候选到最终四元数的回归，并将对称性预测建模为概率分布问题，结合自监督学习策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，SAFAG在性能和鲁棒性方面优于现有方法，尤其在数据稀缺情况下表现突出。

Conclusion: SAFAG有效解决了对称性标注依赖问题，具备广泛应用于具身AI系统的潜力。

Abstract: Urgently needed generalizable robot object interaction and manipulation requires high-quality Cross-Category object perception. As a pioneer of this area, Generalizable and Actionable Parts (GAParts) understanding has attracted increasing attention from relevant researchers. However, most recent works either have insufficient design regarding the symmetry issue or require rich symmetry annotation, which severely impedes precise GAPart pose estimation in data-lacking scenarios. In this paper, we propose SAFAG, a novel Symmetry Annotation-Free framework for Generalizable and Actionable Parts Pose Estimation. Specifically, we suggest a stepwise refinement two-stage framework for candidate-to-final quaternion regression, and tackle the symmetry prediction as a probability distribution problem with self-supervised learning strategy. The experimental results demonstrate the superior performance and robustness of our SAFAG. We believe that our work has the enormous potential to be applied in many areas of embodied AI system.

</details>


### [111] [How to Instruct Your Robot: Dense Language Annotations Power Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17077)
*Bosung Kim,Ruiyi Wang,David Acuna,Jaehun Jung,Alexander Trevithick,Brandon Cui,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeMiAn通过密集多方面语言重标注，提升机器人策略学习的性能，无需新增演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人策略学习中，收集演示数据成本高，而语言标注相对便宜。研究旨在利用语言密度从现有视频数据中提取更多信号。

Method: 提出DeMiAn方法，分两阶段：首先使用VLM生成四个方面的标注（物理动作、场景构成、臂姿态、推理），然后训练一个‘教师’模型，在部署时根据任务描述和初始场景选择合适的标注。

Result: 在超过100万机器人操作片段和5万EgoVerse视频上验证，DeMiAn提升了视觉-语言-动作策略和基于视频的世界-动作模型性能；在RoboCasa上成功率提高5个百分点，接近每任务oracle水平3点内，并改善复合任务和分布外表现。

Conclusion: 密集重标注是一种可行的扩展杠杆，能有效提升机器人策略学习效果，且不依赖新数据采集。

Abstract: Scaling robot policy learning is bottlenecked by the cost of collecting demonstrations, while language annotations for existing demonstrations are comparatively cheap. We study language density as a lever for extracting more signal from a fixed robot or egocentric-video corpus. We introduce DeMiAn (Dense Multi-aspect Annotation), a two-stage approach that first re-labels demonstration segments with VLM-generated annotations along four complementary aspects: physical motion, scene composition, arm pose, and reasoning. A learned instructor then maps a task description and initial scene snapshot to a task-appropriate annotation at deployment, running asynchronously so generation latency is hidden behind policy execution. Across over 1M robot manipulation clips and 50K EgoVerse human-egocentric videos, DeMiAn improves both a vision-language-action policy and a video-based world-action model without collecting new demonstrations. On RoboCasa, the instructor raises success by 5 points over a task-only baseline and comes within 3 points of a per-task oracle. No fixed annotation aspect dominates across tasks, showing that selecting the right dense language matters. DeMiAn also improves composite-task and out-of-distribution performance, and shifts the compute-performance frontier in both mid-training and post-training after accounting for annotation-generation FLOPs. These results position dense re-annotation as a practical scaling lever for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [112] [Contrastive Conceptor Activation Steering (COAST): Unlocking Vision-Language-Action Models through Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17144)
*Miranda Muqing Miao,Subin Kim,Brandon Yang,Lyle Ungar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COAST是一种无需训练的轻量级方法，通过构建“conceptor”线性算子将视觉语言动作（VLA）模型的潜在表示引导至成功子空间，从而显著提升机器人任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型尽管具备强大的感知先验，但在实际机器人任务中仍表现脆弱，常在简单任务上失败，因此需要一种有效且无需重新训练的方法来提升其鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 提出Contrastive Conceptor Activation Steering (COAST)，利用少量成功与失败的任务轨迹数据，构建conceptor以识别任务成功的关键潜在子空间，并在推理时引导VLA的潜变量进入这些成功子空间。该方法适用于多种不同架构的策略模型。

Result: 在三种不同的神经策略模型上，COAST在模拟和真实机器人任务中的平均成功率分别提升了超过20%和40%；发现失败模式在任务间具有共享结构，而成功表征更具任务特异性，且跨任务的相似失败模式可实现概念器的迁移应用。

Conclusion: 当前VLA模型在其潜在空间中保留了大量任务相关知识，性能瓶颈可能源于解码过程；COAST通过引导模型潜流朝向‘成功’分布，提供了一种解锁其潜在能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage powerful perceptual priors from web-scale Vision-Language Model (VLM) pre-training, yet they remain surprisingly brittle in practice, frequently failing at simple robotic tasks. To mitigate this, we propose Contrastive Conceptor Activation Steering (COAST). COAST builds on the notion of a "conceptor", a linear operator that soft-projects data into the principal components of a target distribution. COAST uses conceptors to identify success-critical subspaces for a target robotic task from a few examples of success and failure rollouts. At inference time, it steers VLA latents into these identified success subspaces to improve task outcomes. Across three architecturally distinct neural policies (flow-matching VLA, autoregressive VLA, and Diffusion Policy), COAST improves absolute mean simulation and real-robot task success rate by over 20 and 40% respectively. The activation subspace geometry reveals that failure modes share substantial structure across tasks while success representations remain largely task-specific. When tasks share similar failure modes, this structure enables previously fitted conceptors to improve performance on new tasks without refitting. Ultimately, our results suggest that current VLAs retain substantial task-relevant knowledge in their latent representations, and that the action expert's decoding bottleneck could be mitigated by steering its residual stream toward task-relevant subspaces. COAST provides a lightweight, training-free path to unlocking these latent capabilities by steering the model towards its own "success" distributions.

</details>


### [113] [Event-Grounded Sparse Autoencoders for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17204)
*Xinchen Jin,Aditya Chatterjee,Pranav Kumar,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于事件的稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析管道，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的机械可解释性研究，通过将SAE特征与机器人行为关键事件对齐，实现了在闭环控制下的行为锚定解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言和视觉-语言模型的可解释性工具难以直接应用于VLA策略，因为其输出是机器人动作而非文本，并且干预需通过代价高昂的闭环测试。因此需要一种新的、能与实际行为关联的可解释方法。
